[2026-04-26 14:19:22] VALIDATOR CODE REVIEW
----------------------------------------------------------------------
Review Report:
**Code Review Report: src/components/TimelineItem.tsx**

**Issues Found:**

1. **Type Safety Issue - Discriminated Union Not Properly Enforced**
   - The `TimelineItemProps` discriminated union requires `variant` to be set, but `BaseTimelineProps` makes it optional (`variant?: TimelineVariant`). This creates a scenario where the variant might be undefined at runtime, breaking the discriminated union pattern.
   - **Fix:** Remove the optional `variant` from `BaseTimelineProps` or ensure variant is always provided.

2. **Unnecessary Type Assertions**
   - Multiple lines use type assertions like `(props as ExperienceProps).data.role` which are already guaranteed by the discriminated union. This is redundant and reduces code clarity.
   - **Examples:** Lines extracting `title`, `organization`, `location`, `startDate`, `endDate`, `bullets`, `description`, and `tags`.
   - **Fix:** Leverage the discriminated union properly by narrowing the type once.

3. **Logic Error in Title Construction**
   - Line 48: `(props as EducationProps).data.field` is accessed without checking if it exists. The ternary should use optional chaining or safer access.
   - **Fix:** Use `(props as EducationProps).data.field ? ...` or refactor for clarity.

4. **Missing Accessibility Improvements**
   - The component lacks a `<time>` element wrapping for the mobile date display consistency with the desktop version.
   - The bullet list items use `key={idx}` which is an anti-pattern; should use unique identifiers from data if available.

5. **Responsive Layout Inconsistency**
   - The `isRight` alignment logic applies `md:text-right` and `md:flex-row-reverse` but the bullets and tags use `md:justify-end` conditionally without considering RTL or proper flex alignment on mobile.

6. **Missing Semantic Structure**
   - The desktop date section could be better semantically structured as part of the main article or a `<figure>` element for better screen reader navigation.

7. **Potential Runtime Error**
   - The `formatDateRange()` function assumes valid date strings but doesn't handle invalid date inputs, which could result in "Invalid Date" display.

**Recommendations:**
- Refactor the discriminated union type pattern to eliminate redundant type assertions
- Add proper type narrowing using conditional checks
- Improve date handling with validation
- Use semantic HTML elements (`<time>`, `<figure>`) more effectively
- Replace array indices with stable keys from the data structure

- **Mobile Menu**: Slide-in overlay menu with proper aria attributes and animations
- **Active Section Detection**: Implements scroll position tracking to highlight current section
- **Accessibility**: 
  - Proper ARIA labels, roles, and attributes (aria-label, aria-expanded, aria-controls, role="banner")
  - Focus-visible ring states with proper offsets
  - Semantic HTML structure
  - Keyboard navigation support
- **Utilities**: Uses `cn()` utility from `@/lib/utils.ts` for class management
- **Interactions**: Smooth transitions, click handlers, scroll listeners with passive mode
- **Design System**: Consistent with bright white editorial design (Tailwind color palette: indigo, slate, white)
- **Code Quality**: Proper use of React hooks (useState, useEffect, useCallback), "use client" directive for Next.js
- **Dependencies**: Only adds `react-icons` which is appropriate for icon handling

All imports are correct, component structure is sound, and implementation follows Next.js TypeScript best practices.

Review Report:
**Code Review Report**

**Issue Found:**

1. **Ineffective Solution** - The `webpack` configuration modification does nothing to resolve the favicon route issue. The webpack hook returns the config unmodified, which will not prevent Next.js from attempting to export `/favicon.ico` as a page during static export.

2. **Missing Required Configuration** - To prevent Next.js from resolving `/favicon.ico` as a page route during static export, the configuration needs to implement route exclusion logic. The current approach does not address the root cause.

3. **Incomplete Implementation** - For a static export in Next.js, favicon handling requires either:
   - Explicit configuration to skip favicon routes, OR
   - A proper `rewrites` or `redirects` configuration, OR
   - Use of `skipMiddlewareUrlNormalize` or similar approach

**Recommendation:**

The `webpack` hook should be removed as it serves no purpose here. Instead, implement proper Next.js configuration to exclude favicon from page collection during static export (such as using `experimental.esmExternals` settings or configuring the build to ignore favicon routes explicitly).

The current code will still fail the build as it does not actually solve the `/favicon.ico` resolution problem during static export.
